Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the elliptic object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 434. The moduli space has dimension at most 785 with 5 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_434(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 434. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^785(S, F) is trivial. QED.
